# APC v1.0 follow-up analyses

Frozen tag `v1.0`, commit `71ebc16aed32ca38be73a800738b975e5806b330`. Tagged source and all saved input banks remain byte-identical. New scripts and results are confined to `C:\Users\nghare\.brainstorm\process\revision_checks\v1_followup`. Seed 20260618; MATLAB 9.13.0.2105380 (R2022b) Update 2.

## Factual summary (9 lines)

1. A2: 9,600 scan results on 350 saved main, 150 saved random-fP and 700 saved null inputs, using both phase grids.

2. The copied A1 comparator reproduced all eight frozen metric/grid results exactly on its validation input.

3. Original status-4 diagnosis: 13 main distractor, 14 timing-null and 9 no-coupled-burst trials; 122 new-scenario failures were also traced.

4. All 36 original status-4 trials had zero complete trough epochs after edge rejection; 36 had detected troughs before rejection.

5. No diagnosed refined fP lay outside its padded Welch peak neighbors or the configured 1-30 Hz phase limits.

6. Turning refinement off returned status 0 in 36/36 original diagnosed trials and 122/122 new-scenario diagnosed trials.

7. The slow-rhythm scenario contains 150 coupled trials and 75 paired timing-null trials; all 18 method/grid combinations share each input.

8. Frozen default APC slow-scenario joint recovery totals 58/150.

9. All protected-source, saved-input and paired-null subtraction checks passed; no method changes were applied.

## Definitions, inputs and provenance

A2 amplitude half-width is 12 Hz for every candidate. A1 uses candidate fP. All scans retain the frozen phase half-width 1.5 Hz, amplitude grid 80:8:152, butter(4) bandpass/filtfilt, 18-bin KLMI, GLM R-squared, independent full-grid maxima and first column-major tie handling. Phase grids are 4:1:12 and 4:0.5:12. MVL-raw and conventional Ozkurt-normalized MVL are independently selected scans; Ozkurt = |sum(A exp(i phase))|/sqrt(N sum(A squared)).

Every count has denominator 25. Joint: absolute phase error <=1 Hz and amplitude error <=8 Hz. fA/fP are marginal recovery criteria; strict joint uses phase error <=0.5 Hz. Invalid estimates count as recovery failures; errors use finite estimates, whose counts appear in the summary CSVs. Boundary picks mean fP=4/12 within 1e-9. About-10 picks mean |fP-10|<=1 Hz. APC does not use the comparator phase grid. AUC uses all finite coupled/null score pairs, half weight for ties, without score-direction reversal; finite denominators are shown.

The saved MAT input banks were read directly; no main, random-fP or original null inputs were regenerated. Full paths and SHA-256 hashes: [input_source_manifest.json](<C:/Users/nghare/.brainstorm/process/revision_checks/v1_followup/input_source_manifest.json>). The analysis plan: [PROTOCOL_followup.md](<C:/Users/nghare/.brainstorm/process/revision_checks/v1_followup/PROTOCOL_followup.md>). Trial CSVs and MAT files store the frozen commit, MATLAB version, full APC option inputs, complete comparator settings, generator settings and seed. Initial script hashes: [analysis_manifest.json](<C:/Users/nghare/.brainstorm/process/revision_checks/v1_followup/analysis_manifest.json>); final scripts including the report and independent input check: [analysis_final_manifest.json](<C:/Users/nghare/.brainstorm/process/revision_checks/v1_followup/analysis_final_manifest.json>). All initial MATLAB method/driver script hashes still match. Verification: [verification.json](<C:/Users/nghare/.brainstorm/process/revision_checks/v1_followup/verification.json>).

## Status-4 diagnosis

Complete per-trial values and neighboring bins: [status4_diagnosis.csv](<C:/Users/nghare/.brainstorm/process/revision_checks/v1_followup/status4_diagnosis.csv>). Intermediate arrays and both option runs: [status4_diagnostics.mat](<C:/Users/nghare/.brainstorm/process/revision_checks/v1_followup/status4_diagnostics.mat>). The diagnostic copy reproduced every exported frozen APC result in all 72 refined/unrefined checks.

The diagnosed return is `C:\Users\nghare\.brainstorm\process\apc-brainstorm-publish\src\bst_apc.m:212-214`: `if isempty(phaseLockedAverage) || isempty(phaseLockedTf)`, followed by `metrics.status_code = 4; return;`. The earlier condition at line 202, `isempty(troughLocs)`, did not return any of these trials. The phase-epoch helper rejects a trough when `idx(1) < 1 || idx(end) > numel(signal)`. Its half-window is `max(2, round(phaseEpochCycles*fs/fP))`, with phaseEpochCycles=3. Complete epochs therefore span approximately six phase cycles. The helper returns empty averages when every trough fails that edge condition.

Bounds checks: 0/36 outside the padded peak neighboring bins, 10/36 outside the unpadded peak neighboring bins, and 0/36 outside configured [minPhaseHz,maxPhaseHz]=[1,30]. The padded and unpadded Welch spectra use different frequency grids; their peaks are recorded separately. Parabolic interpolation was actually used in 0/36 cases. All phase bands were valid and all 36 had detected troughs; edge rejection removed every trough.

All 36 refined estimates equal the lowest valid padded Welch bin, approximately 1.007080078125 Hz. Its left neighbor, approximately 0.9765625 Hz, is below minPhaseHz=1. The interpolation guard requires both neighbors to be valid, so it retains the bin frequency in these trials. The refined/unrefined difference therefore arises from the padded spectrum peak search, without a parabolic offset. This half-window is about 2.978 seconds on each side within a six-second record.

Classification: **(b) legitimate failure under the frozen complete-epoch requirement** for all 36. The recorded frequency, trough counts and return path do not identify a refinement-bound or trough-detection bug. Exported fP is NaN on status 4 because the method assigns it only after completing the later stages; the CSV reports the intermediate fP used at Step 3.

Minimal proposed future change, not applied: only when zero complete phase epochs remain, retry Step 2/3 using the existing fPRefine=false setting, then proceed if a complete epoch exists. This guard would be invoked for these 36 diagnosed trials; the existing unrefined runs returned status 0 for 36/36, and 0/36 would retain a failure. The frozen results remain unchanged. These counts concern this diagnosed set only; no count is claimed for unexamined inputs or a redesigned estimator.

### Status 4 in the new slow-rhythm scenario

To include every status-4 case in the new null set, the same diagnostic was also applied to all new-scenario failures, including coupled trials: [slow_status4_diagnosis.csv](<C:/Users/nghare/.brainstorm/process/revision_checks/v1_followup/slow_status4_diagnosis.csv>) and [slow_status4_diagnostics.mat](<C:/Users/nghare/.brainstorm/process/revision_checks/v1_followup/slow_status4_diagnostics.mat>). The proposed guarded unrefined retry would trigger for all 122; 122 existing unrefined runs completed with status 0. No retry was applied to the reported recovery results.

## Slow-rhythm distractor

The scenario uses the frozen distractor generator formulas with true fP=6 Hz, fA=120 Hz, coupling .60, original Gaussian width 28 ms and 10-ms burst-time jitter, sampling 500 Hz, duration 6 seconds and mixed pink/white noise at -3 dB. phase_offset_deg=180 places nominal burst centers at the physical trough of the 6-Hz sine. The full 96-Hz component, including baseline, is omitted. The added 10-Hz sinusoid has amplitude ratio times the 6-Hz amplitude and a trial-wise uniform phase from a separate seed-20260618 stream; it is not event-locked. Original generator draw order, including unused legacy null draws, is retained. These choices were recorded before new outcomes in the protocol.

Ratios in order: 0,0.5,1,1.5,2,3; 25 repeats each. All 18 APC/comparator/grid combinations share each saved input. APC options are unchanged except flipTroughs=false for that requested configuration. Timing nulls at ratios 0,1,3 replace only true-burst timing, using Poisson rate 6 Hz and the original extended event support, a separate seed-20260618 stream, unchanged amplitude/sigma and paired noise. Overlaps are allowed, with no minimum gap. Every paired subtraction residual is <=1e-12. Input cases, components and metadata are saved in six `slow_inputs_level_*.mat` files.
